Mutations are the top-level entry points for creating, updating, and deleting data in the
[GitLab GraphQL API](../../_index.md). For an introduction, see
[Mutations](../../getting_started.md#mutations).

> [!warning]
> Deprecated items are planned to be removed in a
> future release. For more information, see the GitLab
> [deprecation process](../../_index.md#deprecation-and-removal-process).

## Calling a mutation

You call a mutation with one argument, `input`. You put the mutation's arguments inside `input`,
and then request the fields you want returned:

```graphql
mutation($id: NoteableID!, $body: String!) {
  # Arguments go inside `input`.
  createNote(input: { noteableId: $id, body: $body }) {
    # Request the fields you want returned.
    note {
      id
      body
    }
    errors
  }
}
```

Each mutation lists:

- **Input type**: The type of `input`. You need it when you pass all of the arguments as one
  variable, for example `mutation($input: CreateNoteInput!) { createNote(input: $input) { errors } }`.
- **Arguments**: What you can put inside `input`. An argument whose type ends in `!` is required,
  unless it has a default value.
- **Fields**: What you can request in the result. Every mutation returns `errors`, a list of
  error messages. Always request `errors` so you can see why a mutation failed.

### `clientMutationId` {.no_toc}

Every mutation also accepts an optional `clientMutationId` argument of type `String`, and returns
the same value in its result. Use it to match each response to its request. It works the same way
for every mutation, so it is not listed with each mutation's arguments and fields.

## `adminSetDuoAvailability`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.2.

{{< /history >}}

Sets an admin-locked GitLab Duo availability override on a group.

**Input type:** `AdminSetDuoAvailabilityInput`

### `adminSetDuoAvailability` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description | Default |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- | ------- |
| `availability` | [`DuoAvailability!`](enums.md#duoavailability) | GitLab Duo availability state to lock on the group. |  |
| `clearDescendants` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Clear conflicting admin-locked overrides on descendant groups instead of rejecting. | `false` |
| `groupId` | [`GroupID!`](scalars.md#groupid) | Group to set the GitLab Duo availability override on. |  |

### `adminSetDuoAvailability` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `adminLocked` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Whether the group has an admin-locked GitLab Duo availability override. |
| `affectedAdminLockedDescendants` | [`AdminDuoAvailabilityLockedAncestorConnection`](objects.md#adminduoavailabilitylockedancestorconnection) | Descendant groups with admin-locked overrides that must be cleared before this override can be applied. Present only when `clearDescendants` was false; the override was not applied. This field is a [connection](objects.md#connections-and-pagination) and accepts the four standard pagination arguments: `before`, `after`, `first`, `last`. |
| `availability` | [`DuoAvailability`](enums.md#duoavailability) | Resolved GitLab Duo availability for the group after the mutation. |
| `conflictingAncestor` | [`AdminDuoAvailabilityLockedAncestor`](objects.md#adminduoavailabilitylockedancestor) | Ancestor group whose admin lock blocked the mutation, if any. When present, the override was not applied. |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

Defaults to the latest released version. Ignored when enabling within the item's managing project, which always tracks the latest released version. |  |
| `replaceExistingDirectConfigurations` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Confirm replacement of a group configuration, and any project configurations, for this item when inheritance is turned on. Required when the item is already configured for the group. |  |

| `triggerGoals` | [`JSON`](scalars.md#json) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.5.<br/><br/>Goals for the auto-created AI Catalog triggers, keyed by event type. |  |
| `triggerTypes` | [`[String!]`](scalars.md#string) | List of event types to create AI Catalog triggers for (values can be mention, assign or assign_reviewer). |  |

## `aiFlowTriggerCreate`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.3.

{{< /history >}}

**Input type:** `AiFlowTriggerCreateInput`

### `aiFlowTriggerCreate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `active` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.<br/><br/>Indicates whether the AI flow trigger is active. Defaults to true. |
| `aiCatalogItemConsumerId` | [`AiCatalogItemConsumerID`](scalars.md#aicatalogitemconsumerid) | AI catalog item consumer to use instead of config_path. |
| `configPath` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Path to the configuration file for the AI flow trigger. |
| `description` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Description of the AI flow trigger. |
| `eventTypes` | [`[Int!]`](scalars.md#int) | Event types that triggers the AI flow. |
| `filter` | [`JSON`](scalars.md#json) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.0.<br/><br/>Filter conditions for the AI flow trigger. |
| `goals` | [`JSON`](scalars.md#json) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.5.<br/><br/>Goals for the AI flow trigger, keyed by event type. |
| `projectPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Full path of the project the AI flow trigger is associated with. |
| `readyMergeRequestsOnFinish` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.6.<br/><br/>Indicates whether merge requests created by a session that a work item event of the AI flow trigger started are marked as ready when the session finishes. Applies only when the `ai_flow_trigger_status_on_finish` and `ai_flow_trigger_chaining` feature flags are enabled, and only to work items in a project. Requires the `work_item` event type. The work item filter must match all its rules, and have one top-level `action` rule `in` `["status_changed"]` and one top-level `status.name` rule `in` a list of status names. |
| `statusOnFinish` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.6.<br/><br/>Name of the work item status to set when a session that a work item event of the AI flow trigger started finishes. Applies only when the `ai_flow_trigger_status_on_finish` and `ai_flow_trigger_chaining` feature flags are enabled, and only to work items in a project. Requires the `work_item` event type. The work item filter must match all its rules, and have one top-level `action` rule `in` `["status_changed"]` and one top-level `status.name` rule `in` a list of status names. |
| `userId` | [`UserID`](scalars.md#userid) | Service account for the AI flow trigger. |

### `aiFlowTriggerCreate` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `aiFlowTrigger` | [`AiFlowTriggerType`](objects.md#aiflowtriggertype) | Created AI flow trigger. |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.

{{< /history >}}

Activates an Artifact Registry for an organization by claiming a slug.

### `artifactRegistryActivate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `slug` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Slug to claim, Artifact Registry's immutable identifier for the namespace. |

### `artifactRegistryActivate` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `registry` | [`ArtifactRegistry`](objects.md#artifactregistry) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.<br/><br/>The organization's registry: the one just provisioned, or the existing one when the organization is already activated. Null on every other refusal. |

## `artifactRegistryArtifactDelete`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.

{{< /history >}}

Deletes one artifact of an Artifact Registry repository. Permanently deletes a published artifact on a hosted repository and evicts a cached artifact on a remote repository, following the kind of the repository addressed. Artifact Registry accepts the request rather than completing it, so the mutation reports acceptance rather than completion. Re-read the artifact list to see the result.

**Input type:** `ArtifactRegistryArtifactDeleteInput`

### `artifactRegistryArtifactDelete` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `id` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | ID of the artifact in Artifact Registry, as returned by the `id` field on a package or image. Not a GitLab global ID. |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the repository holding the artifact, unique within the organization. |

### `artifactRegistryArtifactDelete` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `repository` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepository`](objects.md#artifactregistryrepository) | Repository holding the deleted artifact. Counters were read before the deletion applied, so they can lag its result. Null when the deletion was not applied. |

## `artifactRegistryContainerTagDelete`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.

{{< /history >}}

Deletes a container tag, identified by its name. Applies only to container repositories in Docker or OCI format. This mutation never reads the repository kind. Artifact Registry decides the outcome from its own records. On a hosted repository, deletion permanently removes the tag. On a remote repository, it evicts the cached tag reference. On a virtual repository, the request is passed through for Artifact Registry to decide. Deleting a tag never removes the manifest it pointed at. Artifact Registry accepts the request rather than completing it, so this mutation reports acceptance, not completion.

**Input type:** `ArtifactRegistryContainerTagDeleteInput`

### `artifactRegistryContainerTagDelete` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `imageId` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | ID of the image holding the tag in Artifact Registry, as returned by the `id` field on an image. Not a GitLab global ID. |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the repository holding the tag, unique within the organization. |
| `tagName` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the tag to delete. Limited to 1024 characters. |

### `artifactRegistryContainerTagDelete` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `repository` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepository`](objects.md#artifactregistryrepository) | Repository holding the deleted tag. A tag delete moves no repository counter. Null when the deletion was not accepted. |

## `artifactRegistryManifestDelete`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.

{{< /history >}}

Deletes a manifest, identified by its digest. Applies only to Docker or OCI container repositories, whose kind this mutation never reads. Artifact Registry decides the outcome from its records, permanently removing the manifest on a hosted repository, evicting the cached copy on a remote one, or passing the request through on a virtual one. Deleting a manifest removes tags pointing at it and index entries where it is the parent, though a manifest it indexed survives untagged. Artifact Registry accepts rather than completes the request, so this reports acceptance. Re-read the image's manifests afterward for the result. Deletion is refused if another manifest indexes the target as a child, and the error lists at most 10 blocking digests plus the total count.

**Input type:** `ArtifactRegistryManifestDeleteInput`

### `artifactRegistryManifestDelete` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `digest` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Content-addressable digest of the manifest, as returned by the `digest` field on a manifest. Limited to 512 characters. |
| `imageId` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | ID of the image holding the manifest in Artifact Registry, as returned by the `id` field on an image. Not a GitLab global ID. |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the repository holding the manifest, unique within the organization. |

### `artifactRegistryManifestDelete` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `repository` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepository`](objects.md#artifactregistryrepository) | Repository holding the deleted manifest. Counters were read before the deletion applied, so they can lag its result. Null when the deletion was not accepted. |

## `artifactRegistryNpmDistTagDelete`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.

{{< /history >}}

Deletes one dist-tag of an npm package in an Artifact Registry repository, addressed by its Artifact Registry ID. Applies to npm repositories only. A non-npm repository is refused before any request reaches Artifact Registry, and the refusal appears in the payload errors. A remote repository is refused the same way, because Artifact Registry holds the dist-tags of a remote repository as a rewritten document rather than as individually addressable rows, so there is no dist-tag row to delete. Removes the dist-tag alone, never the version it named. The mutation reports acceptance rather than completion.

**Input type:** `ArtifactRegistryNpmDistTagDeleteInput`

### `artifactRegistryNpmDistTagDelete` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `id` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | ID of the dist-tag in Artifact Registry. Not a GitLab global ID. |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the repository holding the dist-tag, unique within the organization. |

### `artifactRegistryNpmDistTagDelete` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `repository` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepository`](objects.md#artifactregistryrepository) | Repository holding the deleted dist-tag. A dist-tag delete moves no repository counter. Null when the deletion was not accepted. |

## `artifactRegistryRepositoryCreate`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.3.

{{< /history >}}

Creates a repository in Artifact Registry.

**Input type:** `ArtifactRegistryRepositoryCreateInput`

### `artifactRegistryRepositoryCreate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `description` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Human-readable description of the repository. |
| `format` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepositoryFormat!`](enums.md#artifactregistryrepositoryformat) | Package format the repository holds. Cannot be changed after creation. |
| `kind` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepositoryKind`](enums.md#artifactregistryrepositorykind) | How the repository sources its artifacts. Defaults to hosted in Artifact Registry. |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the repository, unique within the organization. |
| `settings` | [`ArtifactRegistryRemoteSettingsInput`](input_objects.md#artifactregistryremotesettingsinput) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.<br/><br/>Upstream configuration. Required on a remote repository create, rejected on any other kind. |
| `upstreamRepositoryIds` | [`[ID!]`](scalars.md#id) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.5.<br/><br/>Upstream repositories of a virtual repository, as Artifact Registry repository IDs in resolution order. On update, replaces the whole upstream list, and an empty list removes every upstream. Rejected on any other kind. Maximum is 1000 IDs per request. Artifact Registry sets a lower limit on how many upstreams a repository can have. |
| `visibility` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepositoryVisibility`](enums.md#artifactregistryrepositoryvisibility) | Who can read the repository. |

### `artifactRegistryRepositoryCreate` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `refusedUpstreamRepositoryId` | [`ID`](scalars.md#id) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.5.<br/><br/>Artifact Registry ID of the first upstream list entry that Artifact Registry refused. Null unless an entry was refused. |
| `repository` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepository`](objects.md#artifactregistryrepository) | Repository created. Null when the creation was not applied. |

## `artifactRegistryRepositoryTestConnection`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.

{{< /history >}}

Tests the upstream connection of a remote repository in Artifact Registry.

**Input type:** `ArtifactRegistryRepositoryTestConnectionInput`

### `artifactRegistryRepositoryTestConnection` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the repository to test, unique within the organization. |

### `artifactRegistryRepositoryTestConnection` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `httpStatus` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Status the upstream answered the probe with. Null when the probe failed in transport and no response arrived, and when no probe ran. |
| `lastHealthCheckedAt` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Timestamp of the most recent health probe of the upstream, as it reads after this probe. Null when no verdict is recorded, including when this probe's own write did not land. |
| `lastHealthStatus` | [`ArtifactRegistryHealthStatus`](enums.md#artifactregistryhealthstatus) | Stored health verdict as it reads after the probe. Can differ from `passed`, because Artifact Registry moves it to `UNHEALTHY` only once consecutive failures reach its threshold. `UNKNOWN` when no verdict is recorded, including when this probe's own write did not land, and for a status this schema does not recognize. Null when no probe ran. |
| `passed` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates the upstream answered the probe with a status below 500. Reports reachability rather than credential validity, so an upstream 401 or 404 passes. Null when no probe ran. |

## `artifactRegistryRepositoryUpdate`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.3.

{{< /history >}}

Updates a repository in Artifact Registry. Artifact Registry writes the metadata fields and the settings in separate transactions, metadata first, so a failed update can leave a visibility or description change applied while the settings did not change. Re-sending the same mutation converges.

**Input type:** `ArtifactRegistryRepositoryUpdateInput`

### `artifactRegistryRepositoryUpdate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `description` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Human-readable description of the repository. |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the repository to update, unique within the organization. Cannot be changed. |
| `settings` | [`ArtifactRegistryRemoteSettingsInput`](input_objects.md#artifactregistryremotesettingsinput) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.<br/><br/>Upstream configuration. Required on a remote repository create, rejected on any other kind. |
| `upstreamRepositoryIds` | [`[ID!]`](scalars.md#id) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.5.<br/><br/>Upstream repositories of a virtual repository, as Artifact Registry repository IDs in resolution order. On update, replaces the whole upstream list, and an empty list removes every upstream. Rejected on any other kind. Maximum is 1000 IDs per request. Artifact Registry sets a lower limit on how many upstreams a repository can have. |
| `visibility` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepositoryVisibility`](enums.md#artifactregistryrepositoryvisibility) | Who can read the repository. |

### `artifactRegistryRepositoryUpdate` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `refusedUpstreamRepositoryId` | [`ID`](scalars.md#id) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.5.<br/><br/>Artifact Registry ID of the first upstream list entry that Artifact Registry refused. Null unless an entry was refused. |
| `repository` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepository`](objects.md#artifactregistryrepository) | Repository updated. Null when the update was not applied. |

Removes an upstream repository association from a virtual repository in Artifact Registry. The upstream repository and its artifacts remain unchanged, and Artifact Registry compacts the remaining upstream positions. Succeeds even when the association is already gone, or the repository is missing, inaccessible, not virtual, or not of the given format.

| `format` | [`ArtifactRegistryRepositoryFormat!`](enums.md#artifactregistryrepositoryformat) | Package format of the virtual repository. |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the virtual repository holding the upstream repository, unique within the organization. |

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

## `bulkSetVulnerabilityFindingsDueDates`

Bulk set due dates for vulnerability findings using individual UUID entries.
This operation is best-effort: valid updates are applied even if some fail.

**Input type:** `BulkSetVulnerabilityFindingsDueDatesInput`

### `bulkSetVulnerabilityFindingsDueDates` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `projectFullPath` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Full path of the project. |
| `updates` | [`[FindingDueDatesSetInput!]!`](input_objects.md#findingduedatessetinput) | List of finding UUIDs with due dates. A maximum of 1000 updates is allowed per request. |

### `bulkSetVulnerabilityFindingsDueDates` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `assigned` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Number of findings for which a due date was successfully assigned. |
| `errors` | [`[FindingDueDatesSetError!]!`](objects.md#findingduedatesseterror) | Errors encountered during the mutation. A maximum of 1000 errors are returned per request. |
| `removed` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Number of findings for which a due date was successfully removed. |
| `skipped` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Number of findings that were skipped due to errors or not found. |

### `bulkUpdateAiToolRules` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |

### `bulkUpdateAiToolRules` fields {.no_toc}

| `successPath` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Redirect path to use when the response is successful. |

## `createContainerProtectionRepositoryRule`

Creates a repository protection rule to restrict access to a project's container registry.

**Input type:** `CreateContainerProtectionRepositoryRuleInput`

### `createContainerProtectionRepositoryRule` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `minimumAccessLevelForDelete` | [`ContainerProtectionRepositoryRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#containerprotectionrepositoryruleaccesslevel) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 17.11.<br/><br/>Minimum GitLab access level required to delete container images from the container repository. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, the default minimum access level is `DEVELOPER`. Valid only when feature flag `container_registry_protected_containers_delete` is enabled. |
| `minimumAccessLevelForPush` | [`ContainerProtectionRepositoryRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#containerprotectionrepositoryruleaccesslevel) | Minimum GitLab access level required to push container images to the container repository. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, the default minimum access level is `DEVELOPER`. |
| `projectPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Full path of the project where a protection rule is located. |
| `repositoryPathPattern` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Container repository path pattern protected by the protection rule. Must start with the project's full path. For example: `my-project/*-prod-*`. Wildcard character `*` is allowed anywhere after the project's full path. |

### `createContainerProtectionRepositoryRule` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `containerProtectionRepositoryRule` | [`ContainerProtectionRepositoryRule`](objects.md#containerprotectionrepositoryrule) | Container repository protection rule after mutation. |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

## `createContainerProtectionTagRule`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 17.8.

{{< /history >}}

Creates a protection rule to control which user roles can modify container image tags matching a specified pattern.

**Input type:** `createContainerProtectionTagRuleInput`

### `createContainerProtectionTagRule` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `minimumAccessLevelForDelete` | [`ContainerProtectionTagRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#containerprotectiontagruleaccesslevel) | Minimum GitLab access level required to delete container image tags from the container repository. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, no access level can delete tags. |
| `minimumAccessLevelForPush` | [`ContainerProtectionTagRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#containerprotectiontagruleaccesslevel) | Minimum GitLab access level required to push container image tags to the container repository. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, no access level can push tags. |
| `projectPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Full path of the project containing the container image tags. |
| `tagNamePattern` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | The pattern that matches container image tags to protect. For example, `v1.*`. Wildcard character `*` allowed. |

### `createContainerProtectionTagRule` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `containerProtectionTagRule` | [`ContainerProtectionTagRule`](objects.md#containerprotectiontagrule) | Protection rule for container image tags after creation. |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

## `createIssue`

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |

| `createdAt` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Timestamp when the issue was created. Available only for admins and project owners. |
| `description` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Description of the issue. |
| `discussionToResolve` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | ID of a discussion to resolve. Also pass `merge_request_to_resolve_discussions_of`. |
| `dueDate` | [`ISO8601Date`](scalars.md#iso8601date) | Due date of the issue. |
| `epicId` | [`EpicID`](scalars.md#epicid) | Deprecated in GitLab 17.5. This will be replaced by WorkItem hierarchyWidget. |
| `healthStatus` | [`HealthStatus`](enums.md#healthstatus) | Desired health status. |
| `iid` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | IID (internal ID) of a project issue. Only admins and project owners can modify. |
| `iterationCadenceId` | [`IterationsCadenceID`](scalars.md#iterationscadenceid) | Global iteration cadence ID. Required when `iterationWildcardId` is provided. |
| `iterationId` | [`IterationID`](scalars.md#iterationid) | Global iteration ID. Mutually exclusive argument with `iterationWildcardId`. |
| `iterationWildcardId` | [`IssueCreationIterationWildcardId`](enums.md#issuecreationiterationwildcardid) | Iteration wildcard ID. Supported values are: `CURRENT`. Mutually exclusive argument with `iterationId`. iterationCadenceId also required when this argument is provided. |
| `labelIds` | [`[LabelID!]`](scalars.md#labelid) | IDs of labels to be added to the issue. |
| `labels` | [`[String!]`](scalars.md#string) | Labels of the issue. |
| `locked` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates discussion is locked on the issue. |
| `mergeRequestToResolveDiscussionsOf` | [`MergeRequestID`](scalars.md#mergerequestid) | IID of a merge request for which to resolve discussions. |
| `milestoneId` | [`MilestoneID`](scalars.md#milestoneid) | ID of the milestone to assign to the issue. On update milestone will be removed if set to null. |
| `moveAfterId` | [`IssueID`](scalars.md#issueid) | Global ID of issue that should be placed after the current issue. |
| `moveBeforeId` | [`IssueID`](scalars.md#issueid) | Global ID of issue that should be placed before the current issue. |
| `projectPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Project full path the issue is associated with. |
| `statusId` | [`WorkItemsStatusesStatusID`](scalars.md#workitemsstatusesstatusid) | Global ID of the status. |
| `title` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Title of the issue. |
| `type` | [`IssueType`](enums.md#issuetype) | Type of the issue. |
| `weight` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Weight of the issue. |

| ---- | ---- | ----------- |

## `createLatestDiffNote`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.10.

{{< /history >}}

Creates a diff note on a merge request using minimal parameters. SHAs, file paths, and position type are resolved automatically from the latest merge request diff.

**Input type:** `CreateLatestDiffNoteInput`

### `createLatestDiffNote` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `body` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Content of the note. |
| `endNewLine` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | End line number on the new version of the file for a multiline note. When provided, newLine is required and marks the start of the comment range. |
| `endOldLine` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | End line number on the old version of the file for a multiline note. When provided, oldLine is required and marks the start of the comment range. |
| `filePath` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Path of the file to comment on. For renamed files, either the old or new path can be used. |
| `headSha` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | HEAD SHA of the merge request diff. The request fails if it does not match the current diff, guarding against commenting on a stale diff. |
| `internal` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Internal flag for a note. Default is false. |
| `newLine` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Line number on the new version of the file. Use alone for added lines, with oldLine for unchanged context lines. At least one of newLine or oldLine is required. |
| `noteableId` | [`MergeRequestID!`](scalars.md#mergerequestid) | Global ID of the merge request to add a diff note to. |
| `oldLine` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Line number on the old version of the file. Use alone for removed lines, with newLine for unchanged context lines. At least one of newLine or oldLine is required. |

### `createLatestDiffNote` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `note` | [`Note`](objects.md#note) | Note after mutation. |
| `quickActionsStatus` | [`QuickActionsStatus`](objects.md#quickactionsstatus) | Status of quick actions after mutation. |

## `createNote`

Creates a Note.
If the body of the Note contains only quick actions,
the Note will be destroyed during an update, and no Note will be
returned.

**Input type:** `CreateNoteInput`

### `createNote` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `body` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Content of the note. |
| `discussionId` | [`DiscussionID`](scalars.md#discussionid) | Global ID of the discussion the note is in reply to. |
| `internal` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Internal flag for a note. Default is false. |
| `mergeRequestDiffHeadSha` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | SHA of the head commit which is used to ensure that the merge request has not been updated since the request was sent. |
| `noteableId` | [`NoteableID!`](scalars.md#noteableid) | Global ID of the resource to add a note to. |

### `createNote` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `note` | [`Note`](objects.md#note) | Note after mutation. |
| `quickActionsStatus` | [`QuickActionsStatus`](objects.md#quickactionsstatus) | Status of quick actions after mutation. |

## `createPackagesProtectionRule`

Creates a protection rule to restrict access to project packages.

**Input type:** `CreatePackagesProtectionRuleInput`

### `createPackagesProtectionRule` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `minimumAccessLevelForDelete` | [`PackagesProtectionRuleAccessLevelForDelete`](enums.md#packagesprotectionruleaccesslevelfordelete) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 17.10.<br/><br/>Minimum GitLab access required to delete packages from the package registry. Valid values include `OWNER` or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, the default minimum access level is `MAINTAINER`. Available only when feature flag `packages_protected_packages_delete` is enabled. |
| `minimumAccessLevelForPush` | [`PackagesProtectionRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#packagesprotectionruleaccesslevel) | Minimum GitLab access required to push packages to the package registry. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, the default minimum access level is `DEVELOPER`. |
| `packageNamePattern` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Package name protected by the protection rule. For example, `@my-scope/my-package-*`. Wildcard character `*` allowed. |
| `packageType` | [`PackagesProtectionRulePackageType!`](enums.md#packagesprotectionrulepackagetype) | Package type protected by the protection rule. For example, `NPM`, `PYPI`. |
| `projectPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Full path of the project where a protection rule is located. |

### `createPackagesProtectionRule` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `packageProtectionRule` | [`PackagesProtectionRule`](objects.md#packagesprotectionrule) | Packages protection rule after mutation. |

| `spiderTimeout` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Maximum number of minutes allowed for the spider to traverse the site. |  |
| `tagList` | [`[String!]`](scalars.md#string) | Deprecated in GitLab 15.8. Moved to DastProfile. |  |
| `targetTimeout` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Maximum number of seconds allowed for the site under test to respond to a request. |  |
| `useAjaxSpider` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates if the AJAX spider should be used to crawl the target site. True to run the AJAX spider in addition to the traditional spider, and false to run only the traditional spider. | `false` |

| `targetTimeout` | [`Int!`](scalars.md#int) | Maximum number of seconds allowed for the site under test to respond to a request. |
| `useAjaxSpider` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates if the AJAX spider should be used to crawl the target site. True to run the AJAX spider in addition to the traditional spider, and false to run only the traditional spider. |

## `duoSettingsUpdate`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 17.9.

{{< /history >}}

Updates GitLab Duo settings.

**Input type:** `DuoSettingsUpdateInput`

### `duoSettingsUpdate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `aiGatewayTimeoutSeconds` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Timeout for the AI Gateway request. |
| `aiGatewayUrl` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | URL for the local AI Gateway server. |
| `allowAllUnixSockets` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.0.<br/><br/>Whether to allow all Unix sockets for network access. |
| `allowProjectExtension` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.0.<br/><br/>Whether to allow projects to extend the network access domain allowlist. |
| `duoAgentPlatformServiceUrl` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | URL for the local Duo Agent Platform service. |
| `duoCliEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.0.<br/><br/>Indicates whether GitLab Duo CLI access is enabled. |
| `duoCoreFeaturesEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether GitLab Duo Core features are enabled. |
| `includeRecommendedAllowed` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.0.<br/><br/>Whether to include recommended domains in the network access allowlist. |
| `minimumAccessLevelEnableOnProjects` | [`AccessLevelEnum`](enums.md#accesslevelenum) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.7.<br/><br/>Minimum access level for enable on Duo Agent Platform. Ignored if dap_instance_customizable_permissions feature flag is disabled. |
| `minimumAccessLevelExecute` | [`AccessLevelEnum`](enums.md#accesslevelenum) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.7.<br/><br/>Minimum access level for execute on Duo Agent Platform. Ignored if dap_instance_customizable_permissions feature flag is disabled. |
| `minimumAccessLevelExecuteAsync` | [`AccessLevelEnum`](enums.md#accesslevelenum) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.7.<br/><br/>Minimum access level to execute Duo Agent Platform features in CI/CD. Ignored if dap_instance_customizable_permissions feature flag is disabled. |
| `minimumAccessLevelManage` | [`AccessLevelEnum`](enums.md#accesslevelenum) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.7.<br/><br/>Minimum access level for manage on Duo Agent Platform. Ignored if dap_instance_customizable_permissions feature flag is disabled. |
| `selfHostedDuoAgentPlatformServiceSecure` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Whether to use secure transport (TLS) for the local Duo Agent Platform service. |

### `duoSettingsUpdate` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `duoSettings` | [`DuoSettings!`](objects.md#duosettings) | GitLab Duo settings after mutation. |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

## `governPolicyCreate`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.

{{< /history >}}

Creates a policy in the policy store for an organization or group.

**Input type:** `GovernPolicyCreateInput`

### `governPolicyCreate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `actions` | [`[JSON!]`](scalars.md#json) | Actions the policy takes. No more than 1000 actions. |
| `description` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Description of the policy. |
| `groupPath` | [`ID`](scalars.md#id) | Full path of the group whose policy store to use. Exactly one of `organizationId` or `groupPath` is required. |
| `lifecycleState` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Lifecycle state of the policy. |
| `mode` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Enforcement mode of the policy. |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the policy. |
| `organizationId` | [`OrganizationsOrganizationID`](scalars.md#organizationsorganizationid) | Global ID of the organization whose policy store to use. Exactly one of `organizationId` or `groupPath` is required. |
| `policyScope` | [`JSON`](scalars.md#json) | Authored scope of the policy. Mutually exclusive with scopeRego. |
| `rules` | [`[JSON!]!`](scalars.md#json) | Rules of the policy, at least one. No more than 1000 rules. |
| `scopeRego` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Rego expression scoping the policy. Mutually exclusive with policyScope. |
| `triggerType` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Trigger the policy responds to. |

### `governPolicyCreate` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `policy` | [`GovernPolicy`](objects.md#governpolicy) | Policy created in the policy store. |

## `governPolicyUpdate`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.4.

{{< /history >}}

Updates a policy in the policy store for an organization or group. Only the supplied fields are changed; omitted fields keep their current values, while an explicit null clears a nullable field.

**Input type:** `GovernPolicyUpdateInput`

### `governPolicyUpdate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `actions` | [`[JSON!]`](scalars.md#json) | Actions the policy takes. No more than 1000 actions. |
| `description` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Description of the policy. |
| `groupPath` | [`ID`](scalars.md#id) | Full path of the group whose policy store to use. Exactly one of `organizationId` or `groupPath` is required. |
| `lifecycleState` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Lifecycle state of the policy. |
| `mode` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Enforcement mode of the policy. |
| `name` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the policy. |
| `organizationId` | [`OrganizationsOrganizationID`](scalars.md#organizationsorganizationid) | Global ID of the organization whose policy store to use. Exactly one of `organizationId` or `groupPath` is required. |
| `policyId` | [`Int!`](scalars.md#int) | ID of the policy. |
| `policyScope` | [`JSON`](scalars.md#json) | Authored scope of the policy. Mutually exclusive with scopeRego. |
| `rules` | [`[JSON!]`](scalars.md#json) | Rules of the policy, at least one when supplied. No more than 1000 rules. |
| `scopeRego` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Rego expression scoping the policy. Mutually exclusive with policyScope. |
| `triggerType` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Trigger the policy responds to. |

### `governPolicyUpdate` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `policy` | [`GovernPolicy`](objects.md#governpolicy) | Policy updated in the policy store. |

### `groupUpdate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |

| `builtInProjectTemplatesEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.0.<br/><br/>Indicates whether built-in project templates are available for new projects in the group. |
| `duoAutoModeEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.5.<br/><br/>Indicates whether Duo Agent Platform auto mode is enabled for the group. |
| `duoFeaturesEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 16.10.<br/><br/>Indicates whether GitLab Duo features are enabled for the group. |
| `fullPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Full path of the group that will be updated. |
| `lockAiAuditEventsStorageEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.2.<br/><br/>Indicates whether AI audit events storage is enforced for all subgroups and projects. |
| `lockBuiltInProjectTemplatesEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.0.<br/><br/>Indicates if the built-in project templates enabled setting is enforced for all subgroups. |
| `lockDuoAutoModeEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.5.<br/><br/>Indicates if the Duo Agent Platform auto mode setting is enforced for all subgroups. |
| `lockDuoFeaturesEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 16.10.<br/><br/>Indicates if the GitLab Duo features enabled setting is enforced for all subgroups. |
| `lockMathRenderingLimitsEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates if math rendering limits are locked for all descendant groups. |
| `lockToolApprovalForSessionEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.11.<br/><br/>Indicates if the tool approval for session setting is enforced for all subgroups. |
| `mathRenderingLimitsEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates if math rendering limits are used for the group. |
| `name` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the group. |
| `path` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Path of the namespace. |
| `sharedRunnersSetting` | [`SharedRunnersSetting`](enums.md#sharedrunnerssetting) | Shared runners availability for the namespace and its descendants. |
| `stepUpAuthRequiredOauthProvider` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | OAuth provider required for step-up authentication. |
| `toolApprovalForSessionEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.11.<br/><br/>Indicates whether tool approval for session is enabled for the group. |
| `visibility` | [`VisibilityLevelsEnum`](enums.md#visibilitylevelsenum) | Visibility of the namespace. |
| `webBasedCommitSigningEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.2.<br/><br/>Indicates whether web-based commit signing is enabled for the group. |

## `mergeRequestAccept`

Accepts a merge request.
When accepted, the source branch will be scheduled to merge into the target branch, either
immediately if possible, or using one of the automatic merge strategies.

[In GitLab 16.5](https://gitlab.com/gitlab-org/gitlab/-/issues/421510), the merging happens asynchronously.
This results in `mergeRequest` and `state` not updating after a mutation request,
because the merging may not have happened yet.

**Input type:** `MergeRequestAcceptInput`

### `mergeRequestAccept` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description | Default |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- | ------- |
| `commitMessage` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Custom merge commit message. |  |
| `iid` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | IID of the merge request to mutate. |  |
| `projectPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Project the merge request to mutate is in. |  |
| `sha` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | HEAD SHA at the time when the merge was requested. |  |
| `shouldRemoveSourceBranch` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Should the source branch be removed. |  |
| `squash` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Squash commits on the source branch before merge. | `false` |
| `squashCommitMessage` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Custom squash commit message (if squash is true). |  |
| `strategy` | [`MergeStrategyEnum`](enums.md#mergestrategyenum) | How to merge the merge request. |  |

### `mergeRequestAccept` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `mergeRequest` | [`MergeRequest`](objects.md#mergerequest) | Merge request after mutation. |

## `mergeRequestUpdate`

Update attributes of a merge request.

**Input type:** `MergeRequestUpdateInput`

### `mergeRequestUpdate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `description` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Description of the merge request (Markdown rendered as HTML for caching). |
| `iid` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | IID of the merge request to mutate. |
| `mergeAfter` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Date and time after which the merge request can be merged. |
| `mergeBefore` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.6.<br/><br/>Date and time before which the merge request can be merged. |
| `overrideRequestedChanges` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Override all requested changes. Can only be set by users who have permissionto merge this merge request. |
| `projectPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Project the merge request to mutate is in. |
| `removeSourceBranch` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates if the source branch of the merge request will be deleted after merge. |
| `state` | [`MergeRequestNewState`](enums.md#mergerequestnewstate) | Action to perform to change the state. |
| `targetBranch` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Target branch of the merge request. |
| `timeEstimate` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Estimated time to complete the merge request. Use `null` or `0` to remove the current estimate. |
| `title` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Title of the merge request. |

| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `mergeRequest` | [`MergeRequest`](objects.md#mergerequest) | Merge request after mutation. |

| `projectPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Full path of the project that is triggering the pipeline. |
| `ref` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Ref on which to run the pipeline. Ignored when `mergeRequestIid` is provided; the merge request ref path is used instead. |

## `projectCiCdSettingsUpdate`

**Input type:** `ProjectCiCdSettingsUpdateInput`

### `projectCiCdSettingsUpdate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `crossProjectPushForJobTokenAllowed` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates the ability to push to this repository using a job token from an allowlisted project. |
| `displayPipelineVariables` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether pipeline variables can be displayed in the UI. |
| `fullPath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Full Path of the project the settings belong to. |
| `groupRunnersEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether group runners are enabled for the project. |
| `inboundJobTokenScopeEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether CI/CD job tokens generated in other projects have restricted access to this project. |
| `jobTokenScopeEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Deprecated in GitLab 16.0. Outbound job token scope is being removed. This field can now only be set to false. |
| `keepLatestArtifact` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether the latest artifact should be kept for the project. |
| `maxPipelinesPerMergeTrain` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Maximum number of parallel pipelines per merge train. |
| `mergePipelinesEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates if merged results pipelines are enabled for the project. |
| `mergeTrainEnforcement` | [`MergeTrainEnforcement`](enums.md#mergetrainenforcement) | Merge train enforcement level for the project. |
| `mergeTrainsEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates if merge trains are enabled for the project. |
| `mergeTrainsSkipTrainAllowed` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether an option is allowed to merge without refreshing the merge train. Ignored unless the `merge_trains_skip_train` feature flag is also enabled. |
| `pipelineVariablesMinimumOverrideRole` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Minimum role required to set variables when creating a pipeline or running a job. |
| `pushRepositoryForJobTokenAllowed` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates the ability to push to the original project repository using a job token. |
| `resourceGroupDefaultProcessMode` | [`ResourceGroupsProcessMode`](enums.md#resourcegroupsprocessmode) | Default process mode for resource groups in the project. |
| `skipBranchPipelinesForMrs` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether branch pipelines are skipped if a merge request is open. |

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `ciCdSettings` | [`ProjectCiCdSetting!`](objects.md#projectcicdsetting) | CI/CD settings after mutation. |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

## `runnerCreate`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 15.10.

{{< /history >}}

**Input type:** `RunnerCreateInput`

### `runnerCreate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `accessLevel` | [`CiRunnerAccessLevel`](enums.md#cirunneraccesslevel) | Access level of the runner. |
| `description` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Description of the runner. |
| `groupId` | [`GroupID`](scalars.md#groupid) | Global ID of the group that the runner is created in (valid only for group runner). |
| `locked` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates the runner is locked. |
| `maintenanceNote` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Runner's maintenance notes. |
| `maximumTimeout` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Maximum timeout (in seconds) for jobs processed by the runner. |
| `paused` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates the runner is not allowed to receive jobs. |
| `privateProjectsMinutesCostFactor` | [`Float`](scalars.md#float) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 17.7.<br/><br/>Private projects' "compute cost factor" associated with the runner (GitLab.com only). |
| `projectId` | [`ProjectID`](scalars.md#projectid) | Global ID of the project that the runner is created in (valid only for project runner). |
| `publicProjectsMinutesCostFactor` | [`Float`](scalars.md#float) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 17.7.<br/><br/>Public projects' "compute cost factor" associated with the runner (GitLab.com only). |
| `runUntagged` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates the runner is able to run untagged jobs. |
| `runnerType` | [`CiRunnerType!`](enums.md#cirunnertype) | Type of the runner to create. |
| `tagList` | [`[String!]`](scalars.md#string) | Tags associated with the runner. |
| `tokenExpiresAt` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Token expiration time (ISO 8601 format). Must be between 5 minutes and 21 days in the future, and cannot exceed instance/group/project limits. |
| `tokenRotationDeadline` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 18.10.<br/><br/>Deadline for token rotation (ISO 8601 format). Requires tokenExpiresAt. Must be <= tokenExpiresAt. Setting both to the same value disables token rotation. |

### `runnerCreate` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `runner` | [`CiRunner`](objects.md#cirunner) | Runner after mutation. |

| Name | Type | Description | Default |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- | ------- |
| `fromDate` | [`ISO8601Date`](scalars.md#iso8601date) | UTC start date of the period to report on. Defaults to the start of last full month. |  |
| `fullPath` | [`ID`](scalars.md#id) | Filter jobs by the full path of the group or project they belong to. For example, `gitlab-org` or `gitlab-org/gitlab`. Available only to administrators and users with the Maintainer role for the group (when a group is specified), or project (when a project is specified). Limited to runners from 5000 child projects. |  |
| `maxProjectCount` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Maximum number of projects to return. All other runner usage will be attributed to an `<Other projects>` entry. Defaults to 1000 projects. | `1000` |
| `runnerType` | [`CiRunnerType`](enums.md#cirunnertype) | Scope of the runners to include in the report. |  |
| `toDate` | [`ISO8601Date`](scalars.md#iso8601date) | UTC end date of the period to report on. " \ "Defaults to the end of the month specified by `fromDate`. |  |

### `runnersExportUsage` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

Commits the `policy_yaml` content to the assigned security policy project for the given project (`full_path`).

### `scanExecutionPolicyCommit` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `fullPath` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Full path of the project. |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the policy. If the name is null, the `name` field from `policy_yaml` is used. |
| `operationMode` | [`MutationOperationMode!`](enums.md#mutationoperationmode) | Changes the operation mode. |
| `policyYaml` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | YAML snippet of the policy. |
| `projectPath` | [`ID`](scalars.md#id) | Deprecated in GitLab 14.10. Use `fullPath`. |

| `securityPolicyProjectId` | [`ProjectID!`](scalars.md#projectid) | ID of the security policy project. |

### `securityPolicyProjectAssign` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

## `updateContainerProtectionRepositoryRule`

Updates a container repository protection rule that controls who can modify container images based on user roles.

**Input type:** `UpdateContainerProtectionRepositoryRuleInput`

### `updateContainerProtectionRepositoryRule` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `id` | [`ContainerRegistryProtectionRuleID!`](scalars.md#containerregistryprotectionruleid) | Global ID of the container repository protection rule to be updated. |
| `minimumAccessLevelForDelete` | [`ContainerProtectionRepositoryRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#containerprotectionrepositoryruleaccesslevel) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 17.11.<br/><br/>Minimum GitLab access level required to delete container images from the container repository. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, the default minimum access level is `DEVELOPER`. Valid only when feature flag `container_registry_protected_containers_delete` is enabled. |
| `minimumAccessLevelForPush` | [`ContainerProtectionRepositoryRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#containerprotectionrepositoryruleaccesslevel) | Minimum GitLab access level required to push container images to the container repository. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, the default minimum access level is `DEVELOPER`. |
| `repositoryPathPattern` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Container repository path pattern protected by the protection rule. Must start with the project's full path. For example: `my-project/*-prod-*`. Wildcard character `*` is allowed anywhere after the project's full path. |

### `updateContainerProtectionRepositoryRule` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `containerProtectionRepositoryRule` | [`ContainerProtectionRepositoryRule`](objects.md#containerprotectionrepositoryrule) | Container repository protection rule after mutation. |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

## `updateContainerProtectionTagRule`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 17.8.

{{< /history >}}

Updates a protection rule that controls which user roles can modify container image tags matching a specified pattern.

**Input type:** `UpdateContainerProtectionTagRuleInput`

### `updateContainerProtectionTagRule` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `id` | [`ContainerRegistryProtectionTagRuleID!`](scalars.md#containerregistryprotectiontagruleid) | Global ID of the tag protection rule to update. |
| `minimumAccessLevelForDelete` | [`ContainerProtectionTagRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#containerprotectiontagruleaccesslevel) | Minimum GitLab access level required to delete container image tags from the container repository. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, no access level can delete tags. |
| `minimumAccessLevelForPush` | [`ContainerProtectionTagRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#containerprotectiontagruleaccesslevel) | Minimum GitLab access level required to push container image tags to the container repository. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, no access level can push tags. |
| `tagNamePattern` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | The pattern that matches container image tags to protect. For example, `v1.*`. Wildcard character `*` allowed. |

### `updateContainerProtectionTagRule` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `containerProtectionTagRule` | [`ContainerProtectionTagRule`](objects.md#containerprotectiontagrule) | Protection rule for container image tags after creation. |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |

## `updateNamespacePackageSettings`

These settings can be adjusted only by the group Owner.

**Input type:** `UpdateNamespacePackageSettingsInput`

### `updateNamespacePackageSettings` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `auditEventsEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether audit events are created when publishing or deleting a package in the namespace (Premium and Ultimate only). |
| `genericDuplicateExceptionRegex` | [`UntrustedRegexp`](scalars.md#untrustedregexp) | When generic_duplicates_allowed is false, you can publish duplicate packages with names or versions that match this regex. When generic_duplicates_allowed is true, duplicate packages with names or versions that match this regex are rejected. |
| `genericDuplicatesAllowed` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether duplicate generic packages are allowed for the namespace. |
| `lockMavenPackageRequestsForwarding` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether Maven package forwarding is locked for all descendent namespaces. |
| `lockNpmPackageRequestsForwarding` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether npm package forwarding is locked for all descendent namespaces. |
| `lockPypiPackageRequestsForwarding` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether PyPI package forwarding is locked for all descendent namespaces. |
| `lockRubygemsPackageRequestsForwarding` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether RubyGems package forwarding is locked for all descendent namespaces. |
| `mavenDuplicateExceptionRegex` | [`UntrustedRegexp`](scalars.md#untrustedregexp) | When maven_duplicates_allowed is false, you can publish duplicate packages with names or versions that match this regex. When maven_duplicates_allowed is true, duplicate packages with names or versions that match this regex are rejected. |
| `mavenDuplicatesAllowed` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether duplicate Maven packages are allowed for the namespace. |
| `mavenPackageRequestsForwarding` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether Maven package forwarding is allowed for the namespace. |
| `namespacePath` | [`ID!`](scalars.md#id) | Namespace path where the namespace package setting is located. |
| `npmPackageRequestsForwarding` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether npm package forwarding is allowed for the namespace. |
| `nugetDuplicateExceptionRegex` | [`UntrustedRegexp`](scalars.md#untrustedregexp) | When nuget_duplicates_allowed is false, you can publish duplicate packages with names or versions that match this regex. When nuget_duplicates_allowed is true, duplicate packages with names or versions that match this regex are rejected. |
| `nugetDuplicatesAllowed` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether duplicate NuGet packages are allowed for the namespace. |
| `nugetSymbolServerEnabled` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether the NuGet symbol server is enabled for the namespace. |
| `pypiPackageRequestsForwarding` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether PyPI package forwarding is allowed for the namespace. |
| `rubygemsPackageRequestsForwarding` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether RubyGems package forwarding is allowed for the namespace. |
| `terraformModuleDuplicateExceptionRegex` | [`UntrustedRegexp`](scalars.md#untrustedregexp) | When terraform_module_duplicates_allowed is false, you can publish duplicate packages with names that match this regex. When terraform_module_duplicates_allowed is true, duplicate packages with names that match this regex are rejected. |
| `terraformModuleDuplicatesAllowed` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Indicates whether duplicate Terraform packages are allowed for the namespace. |

### `updateNamespacePackageSettings` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `packageSettings` | [`PackageSettings`](objects.md#packagesettings) | Namespace package setting after mutation. |

## `updatePackagesProtectionRule`

Updates a package protection rule to restrict access to project packages. You can prevent users without certain permissions from altering packages.

**Input type:** `UpdatePackagesProtectionRuleInput`

### `updatePackagesProtectionRule` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `id` | [`PackagesProtectionRuleID!`](scalars.md#packagesprotectionruleid) | Global ID of the package protection rule to be updated. |
| `minimumAccessLevelForDelete` | [`PackagesProtectionRuleAccessLevelForDelete`](enums.md#packagesprotectionruleaccesslevelfordelete) | Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 17.10.<br/><br/>Minimum GitLab access required to delete packages from the package registry. Valid values include `OWNER` or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, the default minimum access level is `MAINTAINER`. Available only when feature flag `packages_protected_packages_delete` is enabled. |
| `minimumAccessLevelForPush` | [`PackagesProtectionRuleAccessLevel`](enums.md#packagesprotectionruleaccesslevel) | Minimum GitLab access required to push packages to the package registry. Valid values include `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. If the value is `nil`, the default minimum access level is `DEVELOPER`. |
| `packageNamePattern` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Package name protected by the protection rule. For example, `@my-scope/my-package-*`. Wildcard character `*` allowed. |
| `packageType` | [`PackagesProtectionRulePackageType`](enums.md#packagesprotectionrulepackagetype) | Package type protected by the protection rule. For example, `NPM`, `PYPI`. |

### `updatePackagesProtectionRule` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `packageProtectionRule` | [`PackagesProtectionRule`](objects.md#packagesprotectionrule) | Packages protection rule after mutation. |

Valid values include `DEVELOPER`, `MAINTAINER`, `OWNER`, or `ADMIN`. |

| `keepNDaysAfterDownload` | [`Int`](scalars.md#int) | Keep packages for the period after download. Range is 1-365. Default is 30. |
| `notifyOnFailure` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Whether to notify group owners when cleanup runs fail. Default is `false`. |
| `notifyOnSuccess` | [`Boolean`](scalars.md#boolean) | Whether to notify group owners when cleanup runs succeed. Default is `false`. |

## `vulnerabilityCreate`

**Input type:** `VulnerabilityCreateInput`

### `vulnerabilityCreate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description | Default |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- | ------- |
| `confirmedAt` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Timestamp of when the vulnerability state changed to confirmed (defaults to creation time if status is `confirmed`). |  |
| `description` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Long text section that describes the vulnerability in more detail. |  |
| `detectedAt` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Timestamp of when the vulnerability was first detected (defaults to creation time). |  |
| `dismissedAt` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Timestamp of when the vulnerability state changed to dismissed (defaults to creation time if status is `dismissed`). |  |
| `identifiers` | [`[VulnerabilityIdentifierInput!]!`](input_objects.md#vulnerabilityidentifierinput) | Array of CVE or CWE identifiers for the vulnerability. |  |
| `name` | [`String!`](scalars.md#string) | Name of the vulnerability. |  |
| `project` | [`ProjectID!`](scalars.md#projectid) | ID of the project to attach the vulnerability to. |  |
| `projectTrackedContext` | [`ProjectTrackedContextInput`](input_objects.md#projecttrackedcontextinput) | Tracked context to associate with the vulnerability. |  |
| `resolvedAt` | [`Time`](scalars.md#time) | Timestamp of when the vulnerability state changed to resolved (defaults to creation time if status is `resolved`). |  |
| `scanner` | [`VulnerabilityScannerInput!`](input_objects.md#vulnerabilityscannerinput) | Information about the scanner used to discover the vulnerability. |  |
| `severity` | [`VulnerabilitySeverity`](enums.md#vulnerabilityseverity) | Severity of the vulnerability (defaults to `unknown`). | `UNKNOWN` |
| `solution` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Instructions for how to fix the vulnerability. |  |
| `state` | [`VulnerabilityState`](enums.md#vulnerabilitystate) | State of the vulnerability (defaults to `detected`). | `DETECTED` |

### `vulnerabilityCreate` fields {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
| `vulnerability` | [`Vulnerability`](objects.md#vulnerability) | Vulnerability created. |

## `vulnerabilityDismiss`

### `vulnerabilityDismiss` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |

## `workItemDecisionUpdate`

{{< details >}}

- Status: Experiment

{{< /details >}}

{{< history >}}

- Introduced as an [experiment](../../../../policy/development_stages_support.md#experiment) in GitLab 19.5.

{{< /history >}}

Updates a decision in the decision log of a work item.

**Input type:** `WorkItemDecisionUpdateInput`

### `workItemDecisionUpdate` arguments {.no_toc}

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `description` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Context of the decision. |
| `discussionId` | [`DiscussionID`](scalars.md#discussionid) | Global ID of the originating discussion thread. |
| `id` | [`WorkItemsDecisionID!`](scalars.md#workitemsdecisionid) | Global ID of the decision. |
| `options` | [`[WorkItemDecisionOptionUpdateInput!]`](input_objects.md#workitemdecisionoptionupdateinput) | Existing options of the decision to update. Maximum of 6 options. |
| `question` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Question being decided. |
| `resolutionRationale` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Reasoning for the resolution. Can only be updated on resolved decisions. |
| `resolvedById` | [`UserID`](scalars.md#userid) | Global ID of the user who resolved the decision. Can only be updated on resolved decisions. |
| `resolvingNoteId` | [`NoteID`](scalars.md#noteid) | Global ID of the comment that resolved the decision. Can only be updated on resolved decisions. |
| `selectedOptionContent` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Content of the selected option. Unless exactly one option is selected, a new selected option with this content is added and any selected options are deselected. Can only be updated on resolved decisions. |
| `sourceLink` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | URL of the comment, discussion, or external resource that prompted the decision. |
| `title` | [`String`](scalars.md#string) | Deprecated in GitLab 19.6. Use `question`. |

| Name | Type | Description |
| ---- | ---- | ----------- |
| `decision` | [`WorkItemDecision`](objects.md#workitemdecision) | Decision after mutation. |
| `errors` | [`[String!]!`](scalars.md#string) | Errors encountered during the mutation. |
